## `AUT-1623-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AUT` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Nailing-Machine Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Timber Crate & Pallet Assembly
- **Intro:** Nailing-Machine Operator — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Timber Crate & Pallet Assembly — Verify Machine Isolation and Lockout/Tagout (LOTO): No electrical current (0 V AC) at the control cabinet input terminals; pneumatic pressure must be 0 MPa (atmospheric).

- **Intro:** Crimping-Tool Mechanic — In-situ Removal, Reassembly & Dynamic Tolerance Reset SOP for Wooden Pallet Nailing Machine Maintenance Wear Components — Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) and Energy Isolation: Pneumatic pressure must be 0 bar (0 MPa) before any disassembly. Electrical isolation verified with a voltage tester showing 0 V AC/DC.
- **Key tolerance:** Pneumatic pressure must be 0 bar (0 MPa) before any disassembly. Electrical isolation verified with a voltage tester showing 0 V AC/DC.
- **Standards:** `SANS 10119:2018`, `ISO 12100:2010`, `ISO 13849-1:2015`, `SANS 10252:2018`
